    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
>           raise mapped_exc(message) from exc
E           httpx.ConnectError: [Errno 111] Connection refused

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
>           raise mapped_exc(message) from exc
E           httpx.ConnectError: [Errno 111] Connection refused

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
>           raise mapped_exc(message) from exc
E           httpx.ConnectError: [Errno 111] Connection refused

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
>           raise mapped_exc(message) from exc
E           httpx.ConnectError: [Errno 111] Connection refused

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
>           raise mapped_exc(message) from exc
E           httpx.ConnectError: [Errno 111] Connection refused

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
>           raise mapped_exc(message) from exc
E           httpx.ConnectError: [Errno 111] Connection refused

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
>           raise mapped_exc(message) from exc
E           httpx.ConnectError: [Errno 111] Connection refused

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
>           raise mapped_exc(message) from exc
E           httpx.ConnectError: [Errno 111] Connection refused

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
>           raise mapped_exc(message) from exc
E           httpx.ConnectError: [Errno 111] Connection refused

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
>           raise mapped_exc(message) from exc
E           httpx.ConnectError: [Errno 111] Connection refused

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
>           raise mapped_exc(message) from exc
E           httpx.ConnectError: [Errno 111] Connection refused

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
>           raise mapped_exc(message) from exc
E           httpx.ConnectError: [Errno 111] Connection refused

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
>           raise mapped_exc(message) from exc
E           httpx.ConnectError: [Errno 111] Connection refused

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
>           raise mapped_exc(message) from exc
E           httpx.ConnectError: [Errno 111] Connection refused

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
>           raise mapped_exc(message) from exc
E           httpx.ConnectError: [Errno 111] Connection refused

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
>           raise mapped_exc(message) from exc
E           httpx.ConnectError: [Errno 111] Connection refused

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
